Abstract
We formulateSuper Information Theory (SIT) as a generally covariant effective field theory in mostly-
plus Lorentzian signature (−, +, +, +) in which physical reality is described at low and mesoscopic
energies ( E ≪ Λ UV) by two primitive dynamical informational fields alongside spacetime geometry,
gauge connections, and Standard Model matter: a complex coherence order-parameter field ψ(x) =
|ψ(x)|eiθ(x) with dimensionless coherence ratio Rcoh(x) := |ψ(x)|/ψ0 ≥ 0, and a real temporal-
density scalar ρt(x) governing local operational clock rates. In its authoritative consolidated November
2026 formulation ( M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1, canon v1_1), designated Version 81 to preserve accurate
version chronology after the July 2026 compact corrective V ersion 80lineage (Paper 002, Zenodo DOI
10.5281/zenodo.21693867, July 29, 2026; V ersion 80 Draft 13, July 26, 2026), dynamics are governed
by the single covariant action

Author’s Note on Version 81, Version Chronology, and Model Continuity (November 19,
2026)
Version Chronology and Why This Monograph Is Designated Version 81. Accurate public chronology
separates three distinct stages of the SIT monograph lineage rather than collapsing them into a single
undifferentiated version number:
1. Version 79 (December 18, 2025): The prior full-length monograph baseline whose conceptual
architecture and 2017–2025 priority documentation are preserved for archival continuity.
2. July 2026 Version 80 Compact Corrective Lineage (Papers 002, 048, and Specialized July–
October 2026 Portfolio): The public V ersion 80 designation is already occupied by the July 2026
compact corrective lineage: Paper 048 ( Causal Fermion Systems and Super Information Theory ,
July 28, 2026) cites its frozen comparison source as V ersion 80 Draft 13 dated July 26, 2026,
and Paper 002 ( Super Information Theory: Coherence, Time Density, Informational Dissipation,
and Gravity , 26 pages, published July 29, 2026, Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21693867) is
explicitly cataloged as the correction-integrated V ersion 80 successor to V ersion 79. Across the
same July–October 2026 window, specialized companion notes developed bounded sub-models and
controls: Paper 003 (Level 0 SIT, Penrose–Onsager condensate-source contract), Paper 007 (Super Dark
Time), Paper 014 ( Designing a Coherence-Gravity Test with Bose–Einstein Condensates ), Paper 028
(Cross-Disciplinary SIT Translation Guide ), Paper 033 (finite 2D abelian U (1) lattice toy model),
Paper 034 (SuperTimePosition and Gravitational Phase-Lock), Paper 055 ( QGTCD Constraints Note),
Papers 062–063 ( Physics in Review comparisons), and Paper 114 ( Learning to Feel : initial edition
October 17, 2026; current main paper v1.2 dated October 20, 2026, with technical supplement
intentionally retaining its v1.1 label; pursuing an independent neuroscience track whose neural
synchrony observables are not identified with canonical SIT spacetime fields).
3. November 2026 Version 81 Full Monograph and Machine-Checked Formalization Series:
Because V ersion 80 is already taken by the July 2026 compact corrective lineage, this November 2026
full-length monograph—advancing the frozen November internal working drafts v80.2 (Stage 5.2,
November 16, 2026, commit 8955eab) and v80.3 (Stage 6 plus three Lean 4.30 addenda, Hydrogen

Model-Continuity Contract: July 2026 Version 80 Benchmark vs. November 2026 Version 81
Authoritative Action. The July 2026 compact corrective lineage (Paper 002/003) and the November 2026
authoritative continuum/lattice formalization ( M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 and 4D lattice Models L1–L3) share
research questions, but their actions, field content, and matter couplings are not interchangeable (Table 1).
Every result carried between models must declare its original action, destination action, background,
matter sector, observable, and exact relation type (exact field redefinition, consistent truncation, controlled
approximation, or shared quadratic response calculation); any formula lacking such a bridge remains
attached strictly to its originating model. In particular, Stage 6’s proof that the multiplicative portal
+fSM(Rcoh)Lmatter does not algebraically match a conformal metric unless fSM = 1 does not invalidate
Paper 002’s explicitly conformal July benchmark, and conversely Paper 002’s conformal scalar-tensor
formulas do not automatically become predictions of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1.

in this monograph is classified into five explicit epistemic tiers separating (Tier 1) Lean 4.30 kernel-

7. Bidirectional Corrections and Controls Imported from the July 2026 Intervening Papers
(Version 81 Addition): Importing the strongest mathematical and methodological safeguards
developed across the July 2026 portfolio (Papers 002, 003, 007, 014, 028, and 055), V ersion 81
(a) retracts V ersion 79’s false claim that quantum purity decreases monotonically under arbitrary CPTP
maps (explicitly recording Paper 002’s amplitude-damping and complete-dephasing counterexamples,
restricting universal purity non-increase to unital CPTP channels, and grounding general open-system
contraction in the quantum relative-entropy data-processing inequality D(Φ( ρ)∥Φ( ω)) ≤ D(ρ∥ω),
Section 16.1); (b) separates classical EFT field amplitudes (Rcoh, ρt) cleanly from operational quantum
statistics (purity, visibility, Penrose–Onsager condensate fraction, and point-process event rates)
via open calibration-kernel contracts (Section 2.1); (c) integrates Paper 003/014’s Penrose–Onsager
condensate-source boundary and matched-preparation residual controls ∆ ares = (ahigh−C −alow−C)−
∆ astandard (Section 27); and (d) codifies Paper 002/007’s mass-calibration and finite-source form-
factor identifiability cautions alongside Paper 055’s four standing QGTCD non-implication guardrails
(Section 28).

Part I
Foundations of Super Information Theory
1 Introduction
1.1 Motivation and Theoretical Scope
The foundational postulate of Super Information Theory (SIT) is that “information” is not an abstract Platonic
symbol but an operationally defined property of physical systems, quantified through the local coincidence of
independent physical events. A coincidence event occurs whenever two or more physical processes intersect
within a defined spatiotemporal neighborhood, from the simultaneous arrival of particles in a detector to
the synchronized firing of cortical neurons. The fundamental fields of SIT—the complex coherence field
ψ(x) and the real time-density field ρt(x)—provide the formal mathematical description of the structure and
dynamics of these coincidence processes.
The unification of general relativity and quantum mechanics remains a central challenge of theoretical
physics. Super Information Theory proposes a covariant framework in which quantum phase coherence is not
treated merely as a passive statistical descriptor on a fixed background, but as an active order-parameter field
whose gradients and modulus couple dynamically to spacetime curvature and local proper-time accumulation.
Physical phenomena across gravitational, electromagnetic, and thermodynamic regimes emerge from the
coupled variational dynamics of (ψ, ρt, gµν, Aµ) alongside Standard Model matter, without introducing
unobservable ontological substrates.
Crucially, the core framework developed in this monograph does not claim to constitute an unconditional
ultraviolet-complete quantum theory of gravity at trans-Planckian energies. All continuum field equations
and perturbative loop results are derived and interpreted within a controlled Wilsonian effective field theory
(EFT) regime valid below an explicit ultraviolet cutoff Λ UV := min(MPl, Λ EM, Λ sym); whether SIT admits
a regulator-independent non-perturbative 4D gravitational UV fixed point above Λ UV remains an explicit
open problem (OPEN-1, Appendix Z.5).
Within this well-defined effective regime, gravity, electromagnetism, and quantum interference represent
distinct geometric aspects of a unified informational field structure:
• Gravity is sourced by the stress–energy of matter together with the coupled dynamics of the time-density
scalar ρt(x) and the gauge-invariant coherence modulus Rcoh(x) := |ψ(x)|/ψ0.
• Electromagnetism is realized through a U (1) principal bundle connection Aµ(x) coupled minimally to
the coherence phase θ(x) ≡ arg(ψ(x)) via Dµψ = ( ∇µ − iˆqAµ)ψ, reducing on flat pure-gauge patches
(Fµν = 0) to Aharonov–Bohm phase holonomy while retaining two physical transverse polarizations (plus
a longitudinal Proca mode when v0 > 0) globally.
The central objective of this monograph is to establish this unified canonical action ( M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1),
prove its mathematical stability, ghost freedom, curved3+1 D ADM constraint closure, and non-perturbative
4D lattice gauge consistency, demonstrate its exact reduction to General Relativity, Maxwell/Proca theory,
quantum mechanics, and kinetic theory in tested limits, and derive concrete laboratory falsifiers in optical-
clock metrology, cold-atom interferometry, and 87Rb Bose–Einstein condensates.
23

--- PAGE 24 ---
1.2 Foundational Insight: From Neuroscience to a Unified Physical Theory
The conceptual architecture of Super Information Theory grew out of a synthesis uniting two inverse scaling
laws across neuroscience and fundamental physics. This synthesis provided the bridge for extending an
oscillatory theory of mind ( Self-Aware Networks) into a covariant field theory of spacetime and coherence,
beginning in 2022 with Quantum Gradient Time Crystal Dilation (QGTCD) .
In systems neuroscience, the 1/f power law (spectral “pink noise”) characterizes cortical population
activity in which oscillatory amplitude scales inversely with temporal frequency: slower, large-scale rhythms
coordinate broad spatial ensembles, whereas faster, localized bursts carry rapid state updates. In fundamental
wave mechanics, wavelength λ and period T likewise scale inversely with frequency f . Synthesizing these
observations yields an organizing physical principle: at fixed informational energy budget, a coherent wave
configuration trades off spatial/structural Magnitude against dynamical Frequency.
Visualized through the physiological contrast between sustained tonic synchronization and rapid phasic
signaling, this trade-off motivates an informational reading of Einstein’s mass–energy equivalenceE = mc2
across distinct morphologies of coherent organization:
• Radiative Energy ( E): Coherent excitations organized as high-frequency, propagating, low-amplitude
modes (analogous to phasic bursts).
• Localized Mass ( m): Coherent excitations organized as persistent, phase-locked, spatially localized
standing structures—informational solitons or “time crystals” (analogous to tonic attractors)—whose
coherence modulus thickens local time density ρt.
• Decoherent Vacuum Baseline: The reference state (ρt = ρ0, R coh = R0) relative to which gradients of
coherence and time density source measurable curvature and clock shifts.
1.3 Relation to Causal Fermion Systems and Bounded Comparison Scope
An instructive point of comparison in foundational physics is the relationship between Super Information
Theory (SIT) and Causal Fermion Systems (CFS), developed by Felix Finster and collaborators from operator
algebras and a causal action principle. Following the bounded four-lens audit established in Paper 048
(Causal Fermion Systems and Super Information Theory , July 28, 2026), V ersion 81 strictly separates dated
conceptual parallels from mathematical equivalence or empirical validation:
• Dated Conceptual Parallels (Narrative Level Only): SIT’s 2017 premise framing information
around relational “coincidence patterns” and internal distributed observation (NAPOT) shares high-
level vocabulary with later 2024–2025 CFS expositions describing spacetime as a “web of correlations”
and intrinsic “effective dynamical collapse.” Chronological overlap in conceptual framing is not a
mathematical embedding, a proof of operational equivalence, or an independent empirical validation of
SIT.
• Bounded Effective-Equation Overlap and Null Control (Paper 048): Under Paper 048’s frozen
comparison against July 2026 V ersion 80 Draft 13, both a regulated constant-coupling CFS continuum
regime and a specialized neutral-Dirac constant-background SIT submodel reduce to standard Einstein–
Dirac equations, matching a direct Einstein–Dirac null control with zero discriminatory value .
Extending that constant-background truncation check to the November 2026 multiplicative action
M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 remains a separate open calculation.

1.4 Relation to Prior Work and Technical Comparison with Unification Frameworks
1.4.1 From Static Bits to Dynamical Coherence
Information theory entered physics in modern quantitative form through Claude Shannon’s 1948 measure
of symbolic uncertainty, H = − ∑
i pi log pi, defined over discrete probability distributions on a timeless
register. While foundational to communication engineering, combinatorial symbol counting says nothing
about continuous phase relationships inside physical media. John Wheeler’s “It from Bit” shifted attention
toward binary physical interrogations, and Wojciech Zurek’s environment-induced decoherence program
demonstrated how open-system coupling selects pointer states by exporting phase correlations into an
environment.
Super Information Theory embeds these motivations into a local covariant field theory by introducing two
dynamical EFT variables: a complex order parameter ψ(x) with amplitude ratio Rcoh(x) = |ψ(x)|/ψ0 and
a real scalar ρt(x) (kept distinct from quantum purity and event-rate proxies via open calibration kernels,
Section 2.1). Closed-system global U (1) invariance yields the conserved Noether current J µ
coh (distinct from
operational open-system purity, which is non-increasing specifically under unital CPTP maps while general
CPTP channels contract quantum relative entropy, Section 16), and thermodynamic-gravity analogies are
investigated as coarse-grained effective comparisons rather than identities.

acquire a concrete local field-theoretic realization inside Super Information Theory through the coupled
dynamics of Rcoh(x) and ρt(x).

RTI’s Handshake as Phase Alignment
In transactional language, a completed quantum event corresponds to phase matching between offer and
confirmation amplitudes. Within SIT, local phase alignment is measured by the vanishing of relative phase
mismatch ∆ θ(x) = arg( ψret) − arg(ψadv), maximizing local interference visibility and rendering the
coherence current J µ

SIT tracks how constructive phase alignment elevates Rcoh(x) and, via the linkage potential Ulink(ρt, |ψ|),
shifts the local time-density scalar ρt(x) in strictly causal, hyperbolic fashion.
Entropic Gravity as a Coarse-Grained Limit
In entropic gravity, displacing a test mass m by ∆ x near a holographic screen of temperature T changes
screen entropy by ∆ S = (2 πkB/ℏ)mc∆ x, yielding F ∆ x = T ∆ S. In SIT, a localized rise in Rcoh shifts
ρt through Ulink and dresses matter stress–energy via fSM(Rcoh), generating a screened Y ukawa addition
to the Einstein-frame Newtonian potential Φ E(r) = −GfSM0M/r. Coarse-graining over microscopic
phase modes reproduces thermodynamic force balance without sacrificing local stress–energy conservation

Quantum Coherence as Information Density and Quasicrystal Echoes
Regions whose constituent modes share a common phase exhibit elevated Rcoh and lowered linkage
potential energy. At fixed mode energy, the inverse amplitude–frequency trade-off implies that phase-locked,
high-amplitude configurations oscillate at lower effective coordinate rates, corresponding operationally
to gravitational redshift. Across scales, constructive and destructive interference domains in (Rcoh, ρt)
play a geometric role analogous to quasiperiodic projections in optical and plasmonic lattices: coherent
condensations deepen gravitational wells while decoherent expanses relax toward the vacuum baseline.
2.1 Operational and Mathematical Definitions of Primitive Fields

complex scalar field of mass dimension[M ]1 (in natural units ℏ = c = 1) on the four-dimensional Lorentzian

with covariant derivative Dµψ = ( ∇µ − iˆqAµ)ψ. We emphasize that ψ(x) is not a single-particle
quantum-mechanical Schrödinger wavefunction and |ψ(x)|2 is not a Born probability density; rather, ψ(x)
is a macroscopic covariant order parameter in the effective-field-theory sense (analogous to the Ginzburg–
Landau condensate order parameter in superconductivity or superfluidity), quantifying coherent structural
order available to dress matter and gauge couplings.

3 Operational Definitions and the Informational Fields
A central methodological commitment of Super Information Theory is that “information” is treated as an
operationally instantiated physical structure rather than a disembodied metaphysical substance. Its physical
realization is mediated by concrete degrees of freedom whose interactions generate measurable events.
Accordingly, Rcoh(x) and ρt(x) do not postulate unobservable Platonic entities acting outside physical
law; rather, they furnish covariant field variables in M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 whose connection to concrete
laboratory observables (atomic clock frequency shifts, torsion-balance torques, interferometric visibilities,
or condensate fractions) is governed explicitly by the open calibration and source-to-apparatus contracts of
OPEN-6 (Paper 002, Section 4; Section 28).
No claim is made that abstract informational notation is ontologically prior to physical degrees of freedom,
nor that renaming a laboratory measurement (such as purity or clock rate) by a field symbol establishes
a new dynamical field. Instead, following the calibration checklist of Paper 002 (Section 4.4), the field
interpretation becomes empirically informative only when an explicit state-to-field map compresses multiple
measurements, transfers out of sample across apparatuses or preparations, and predicts a reversible residual
beyond established general-relativistic, electromagnetic, thermal, and mechanical systematics.

4.4 Relativistic Clarification: Internal vs. External Coherence Perspectives
External laboratory observers see ultrarelativistic wavepackets cycle rapidly in coordinate time ( ∆ t small),
whereas in the particle’s comoving proper frame ( ∆ τ = γ−1∆ t) internal phase evolution is Lorentz-dilated,
preserving ∆ E ∆ t ≥ ℏ/2. Because ρt(x) is a genuine Lorentz scalar rather than a coordinate time axis,
SIT requires no multitemporal signature (−, −, +, +) (which would introduce fatal Ostrogradsky/kleinian
ghosts, purged as branch C05 under ODQ-2): proper causality is governed everywhere by a single Lorentzian
metric gµν of signature (−, +, +, +).
In phenomenological regimes where ρt tracks Rcoh monotonically around ρ0, one may write the smooth
adiabatic representation ρt(x) ≈ ρ0 exp[αRcoh(x)] = ρ0[1 + αRcoh(x) + O(α2)], or equivalently via

5.1 Primitive Fields, Spacetime Conventions, and Dimensional Ledger
The authoritative continuum effective field theory M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 (canon v1_1, locked across ODQ-1
through ODQ-7) is defined on a four-dimensional time-oriented Lorentzian manifold (M4, gµν) in mostly-
plus metric signature (−, +, +, +) with Minkowski reference ηµν = diag( −1, +1, +1, +1) and natural
units ℏ = c = 1 ([M ] = GeV). All field and parameter mass dimensions are locked strictly as follows:
• Dimension [M ]−2: Gravitational coupling κG := 8πG = M −2
Pl > 0.
• Dimension [M ]0 (dimensionless): Metric tensor gµν, dimensionless coherence ratio Rcoh := |ψ|/ψ0 ≥ 0,
vacuum coherence ratio R0 := v0/ψ0 ≥ 0, electromagnetic gating coefficient cEM ≥ 0, dimensionless
symmetron scale parameter Msym > 0, quartic self-couplings λρ > 0 and λψ > 0, portal coupling
λportal ≥ 0, U (1) charge coupling ˆq ≥ 0, and positive kinetic normalizations κt > 0, κc > 0.
• Dimension [M ]1 (GeV): Real temporal-density scalar ρt, complex coherence scalar ψ = u + iv = |ψ|eiθ,
U (1) gauge connection Aµ with curvature Fµν = ∂µAν − ∂νAµ and covariant derivative Dµψ = ( ∂µ −
iˆqAµ)ψ, together with reference scales ψ0 > 0, ρ0 > 0, v0 = ψ0R0 ≥ 0, mlink > 0, MPl, Λ EM, Λ sym,
and Λ UV.
• Dimension [M ]4 Lagrangian densities: LEH, Lcoh, Lcoh

• Dimension [M ]6 EFT operators: − cEM

8 Action Principle and Field Equations
8.1 Symmetries and Minimal Canonical Model Selection (Not Uniqueness)
The canonical action SAUTH is a specific minimal ghost-free ansatz —not the unique action permitted
by symmetry—chosen within the class of dimension- ≤ 4 operators (plus leading Z2-even gauge-
invariant dimension-6 dressings fEM, fSM) satisfying four baseline requirements: (1) four-dimensional
diffeomorphism invariance; (2) local U (1) gauge invariance ψ ↦→ eiˆqχψ, Aµ ↦→ Aµ + ∂µχ; (3) discrete
C, P, T invariance and absence of bare phase-dependent potentials; and (4) strict second-order hyperbolicity
excluding higher-derivative Ostrogradsky ghosts such as (□ρt)2 or (∇µ∇νRcoh)2 (C07 purged under
ODQ-2). Because Ulink explicitly breaks ρt ↦→ − ρt symmetry via mlink(ρt − ρ0), symmetry and second-
order hyperbolicity alone still permit additional dimension-≤ 4 operators—including non-minimal curvature
couplings (ξ1Rρt, ξ2Rρ2
t , ξ3R|ψ|2), odd polynomial terms ( ρt, ρ3
t , |ψ|2ρt), and independent quartic cross-
terms |ψ|2ρ2

than claimed symmetry uniqueness.

Noether charge conservation from operational quantum basis coherence, density-matrix purity, entanglement,
and neural synchronization (following the operational separation contract of Section 2.1 and Paper 002).

Exact Scope of Noether Charge Conservation vs. Operational Coherence Measures: The conservation
law ∇µJ µ
coh = 0 is an exact on-shell theorem strictly for the global U (1) Noether charge Qcoh =∫
Σ J 0
coh
√q d3x of the complex EFT field ψ in M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1. It does not imply conservation or
monotonicity of arbitrary operational coherence statistics:
• What Noether Conservation Establishes: In a closed classical solution of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 (with
appropriate boundary falloff), total U (1) Noether charge Qcoh is conserved, and local charge changes are
balanced by spatial flux ∇ · Jcoh.
• What Noether Conservation Does Not Establish: Conservation of Qcoh does not prove conservation of
fixed-basis quantum off-diagonal coherence, density-matrix purity, multipartite entanglement, or neural
cross-frequency synchrony. Under unitary quantum evolution, fixed-basis coherence is not conserved: for
example, a single-qubit Hadamard unitary maps |0⟩ ↦→ | +⟩, changing computational-basis off-diagonal
coherence 2|ρ01| from 0 to 1 while purity Tr(ρ2) = 1 stays constant. Similarly, under non-unital open-
system CPTP maps (Section 16), even purity can strictly increase.
9.3 Why Uncertainty Relations Do Not Establish a Universal Coherence Budget
Earlier drafts of SIT proposed a universal conservation-of-coherence rule across complementary observables
(A, B) of the schematic form CACB ≳ χ > 0, attempting to derive the Heisenberg–Robertson uncertainty
principle (∆ x ∆ p ≥ ℏ/2) and neural cross-frequency trade-offs as instances of a single conserved coherence
budget. V ersion 81 explicitly retracts any claim of a universal conserved coherence budget or universal
positive product lower bound across complementary bases:
1. Counterexample to Universal Lower Bound CACB ≥ χ > 0: For a single qubit in the maximally
mixed state ρ = I/2, off-diagonal coherence vanishes identically in every orthonormal basis ( CZ =
CX = CY = 0 ), so CACB = 0 for every complementary pair, refuting any universal positive lower
bound χ > 0 on basis coherence measures.
2. Counterexample to Unitary Basis-Coherence Conservation: A unitary transformation (such as
the Hadamard gate |0⟩ ↦→ | +⟩) changes fixed-basis coherence freely while leaving purity invariant,
showing that unitary quantum dynamics conserves inner products and von Neumann entropy rather
than a single fixed-basis coherence functional.
3. Distinction Between Variance Uncertainty and Coherence Conservation: Standard quantum
mechanics derives Robertson–Schrödinger variance bounds ( ∆ A ∆ B ≥ 1
2 |⟨[A, B]⟩|) and entropic
uncertainty relations from non-commutativity of observables on Hilbert space. Those established
theorems constrain simultaneous sharpness in incompatible bases, but do not follow from U (1) Noether
charge conservation of a classical EFT field ψ, nor do they derive neural band-power trade-offs (which
belong to separate empirical neuroscience modeling, Section 36).
The motivating conceptual research question—how basis-dependent coherence, entanglement, and
measurement-induced disturbance trade off inside specified quantum protocols, and whether cross-frequency
synchrony exhibits empirical trade-offs in neural recordings—remains part of the interdisciplinary research
program, kept cleanly separated from the proved Noether current ∇µJ µ
coh = 0 of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1.

11 SIT 3.0: Exploratory State-Dependent Clock Hypothesis and
Impossibility of a Linear Coherence Operator
This section preserves the exploratory “SIT 3.0” research question—whether quantum coherence or
entanglement in a localized register could enter an effective proper-time rate—while correcting a fatal
mathematical inconsistency in earlier drafts (which postulated a single fixed Hermitian operator ˆRcoh with
mutually incompatible properties). This hypothesis is a separate phenomenological extension , not derived
from unchanged M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1.
Mathematical Impossibility of the Earlier Linear Operator ˆRcoh. Earlier SIT drafts simultaneously
required a fixed, state-independent Hermitian operator ˆRcoh to satisfy: (a) Tr(ρ ˆRcoh) = 2 |ρ01| on single-
qubit density matrices, (b) ˆRcoh|ψ1⟩ ⊗ · · · ⊗ | ψN ⟩ = 0 on every N -partite product state, and (c) positive
eigenvalues Nc > 0 and Nw > 0 on GHZ and W states. Two elementary proofs establish that no such linear
operator exists:
1. Violation of Linearity Under Mixing: Both |+⟩ = ( |0⟩ + |1⟩)/
√
2 and |−⟩ = ( |0⟩ − | 1⟩)/
√
2 satisfy
2|ρ01| = 1 . Their equal statistical mixture is ρ = 1
2 |+⟩⟨+| + 1
2 |−⟩⟨−| = I/2, which has 2|ρ01| = 0 .
Because the expectation value Tr(ρ ˆO) of any fixed linear observable ˆO is affine in ρ, having expectation
1 on both |+⟩ and |−⟩ forces expectation 1 (not 0) on I/2. Thus 2|ρ01| is a nonlinear convex state
functional, never the expectation value of a single state-independent Hermitian operator.
2. Computational Product Basis Forces ˆRcoh ≡ 0: The 2N computational product states {|i1⟩⊗· · ·⊗|iN ⟩ :
ik ∈ {0, 1}} form a complete orthonormal basis of (C2)⊗N . Any linear operator that annihilates every
product state therefore vanishes identically ( ˆRcoh = 0 ) and must also annihilate every superposition
thereof, including (|0⟩⊗N + |1⟩⊗N )/
√
2 and |WN ⟩; it cannot possess nonzero GHZ or W eigenvalues.

Corrected Exploratory Formulation (Nonlinear State Functional vs. Restricted Linear Witness).

Open Status of Downstream Clock, Sorkin ( I123), and No-Signaling Claims. Because the earlier

Cstate[ρ] (or witness) and microscopic coupling γ are specified ( OPEN-6C). Furthermore, V ersion 81
retracts the assertion that a “phase-only” state-dependent modification at O(γ) automatically guarantees
no-signaling: in nonlinear modifications of quantum mechanics, preventing superluminal signaling requires
specifying the full composite-system state-update law, tensor-product extension, and measurement/collapse
prescription explicitly (cf. Gisin–Polchinski nonlinear quantum mechanics constraints). In the γ → 0 limit,
standard quantum mechanics and General Relativity are recovered identically.

15.1 Exploratory Tunneling-Rate Analogy
In standard quantum mechanics, one-dimensional barrier transmission is governed by the stationary
Schrödinger equation −(ℏ2/2m)ψ′′ + [V (x) − E]ψ = 0 . Earlier SIT drafts explored a phenomenological
analogy in which an effective barrier stiffness or inverse diffusion parameter depends on local time
density, −D(ρt) u′′ + [ V (x) − E]u = 0 with WKB exponent 2
∫ x2
x1
√
(V (x) − E)/D(ρt(x)) dx.
Note that this stationary equation is a heuristic phenomenological model (not a direct consequence of
M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1). Moreover, V ersion 81 corrects a sign error in earlier drafts: if D(ρt) ∝ 1/ρt so that
the WKB action scales as
∫ √
(V − E)ρt dx, then increasing ρt increases the WKB barrier exponent and
suppresses (rather than enhances) transmission T ∼ exp(−2Seﬀ /ℏ); conversely, enhancing transmission at
larger ρt would require D(ρt) to increase with ρt. Any physical SIT modification to tunneling rates remains
an open Level-1 phenomenology question ( OPEN-6C).
15.2 Time–Energy Uncertainty Bounds and Consistency Check
The quantum time–energy uncertainty relation ∆ E ∆ t ≥ ℏ/2 (in Mandelstam–Tamm operational form,
relating energy spread to the minimum timescale for orthogonal state evolution) is a standard kinematic
theorem of Hilbert-space quantum mechanics. Earlier SIT drafts heuristically substituted ∆ t ≈ ρ−1
t together
with ∆ E ≈ Ecoh(1 − Rcoh) while simultaneously associating high coherence ( Rcoh → 1) with large ρt
and “greater temporal uncertainty.” V ersion 81 explicitly retracts that heuristic combination as internally
inconsistent:
1. If ∆ t = ρ−1
t , then larger ρt gives smaller (not greater) ∆ t;
2. Taking Rcoh → 1 together with large ρt drives the product Ecoh(1 − Rcoh)ρ−1
t → 0, violating ≥ ℏ/2
(and conversely rearranging the inequality asρt ≤ 2Ecoh(1−Rcoh)/ℏ would force ρt → 0 as Rcoh → 1,
contradicting the manuscript’s own high- ρt / high-Rcoh correlation).
Standard quantum uncertainty relations therefore stand on their own Hilbert-space derivation and are neither
replaced by nor derived from the classical EFT scalar fields (ρt, ψ).

15.5 Distinction from Position–Momentum Uncertainty
Standard canonical position–momentum uncertainty ∆ x ∆ p ≥ ℏ/2 and Mandelstam–Tamm time–energy
bounds belong to quantum kinematics, whereas the classical SIT EFT fields (ρt, ψ) govern covariant field
dynamics at scales below Λ UV; V ersion 81 keeps these two domains cleanly separated rather than equating
ρ−1
t with a quantum measurement duration.
15.6 Singularity Avoidance Remains an Open Ultraviolet Question
Because the earlier heuristic inequality ρt ≤ 2Ecoh(1 − Rcoh)/ℏ is internally inconsistent with Rcoh → 1
at large ρt (Section 15.2), V ersion 81 retracts the claim that this algebraic rearrangement prevents classical
gravitational singularities. Within the effective field theory M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1, curvature behavior above
the cutoff scale Λ UV lies outside the EFT regime of validity, and singularity resolution remains an open
ultraviolet quantum-gravity problem ( OPEN-5).
15.7 Light, Lasers, and the Gravitational Field
Coherent light, such as that produced by a laser, represents a macroscopic quantum state in which photons are
phase-aligned and occupy a well-defined mode. In conventional physics, the gravitational field generated by
a laser is expected to be minuscule, scaling only with the energy of the beam and independent of its coherence
properties. This expectation has been formalized in calculations showing that a 1 J laser pulse, regardless
of coherence, generates the same infinitesimal spacetime curvature as an equivalent incoherent light pulse
with the same energy distribution. Classical general relativity therefore treats coherence as organizational,
not causal: it simply concentrates energy, but does not amplify or uniquely source gravity.
In contrast, SIT proposes that coherence—when maximized among a system of photons—induces localized
variations in the time-density field, ρt, which couple directly to spacetime curvature. The hypothesis is
that the more perfectly phase-aligned a quantum system, the more efficiently it modulates or “focuses” the
informational content of its stress-energy, leading to enhanced or anomalous gravitational effects not captured
by classical GR.
15.8 Bose–Einstein Condensates and Coherence-Induced Gravity
Matter-wave coherence is realized most dramatically in Bose–Einstein condensates (BECs), where a
macroscopic population of atoms occupies the same quantum state. In standard physics, the gravitational
field produced by a BEC is again expected to reflect only its total mass-energy; coherence is not considered
as a causal modifier. SIT, however, extends its conjecture to matter systems: the perfect coherence of a
BEC is posited to alter local time-density gradients, potentially generating subtle deviations in gravitational
behavior compared to an incoherent ensemble of the same mass.
If SIT is correct, one would expect ultra-coherent systems—whether composed of photons or massive
particles—to generate or modulate gravitational fields in ways that cannot be reduced to energy density
alone. This prediction sets the stage for experimental challenge.
57

--- PAGE 58 ---
15.9 Experimental Evidence and Proposals
To date, no experiment has definitively observed gravitational anomalies attributable to coherence per se.
The gravitational field of laboratory light beams, including intense lasers, remains below current detection
thresholds, and all observed phenomena are consistent with classical predictions. Likewise, tests with
BECs have not revealed deviations from the equivalence principle: coherent matter falls at the same rate
as incoherent matter, within experimental error.
Nevertheless, advancing technology is narrowing the gap between SIT predictions and empirical accessibility.
Proposed experiments include:
• High-power laser interferometry to search for self-induced gravitational lensing or beam–beam interactions
exceeding classical expectations.
• Drop tests comparing the free-fall acceleration of coherent BECs versus thermal clouds under ultra-
sensitive gravimetry.
• Quantum tomography and interferometric protocols measuring declared operational coherence statistics
(purity, visibility, or condensate fraction) under matched-preparation controls (Eq. ( 98)) once an explicit
state-to-field map (OPEN-6C) is specified.
Furthermore, proposed gravity-mediated entanglement experiments (such as BMV-type setups) probe
whether gravitational fields act quantum-mechanically on spatial superpositions; testing any SIT-specific
contribution beyond standard linearized quantum gravity requires deriving the corresponding observable
shift from a specified action and parameter point ( OPEN-6).
15.10 Scoped Falsifiability
Consistent with the Scoped Falsification Principle (Section 28), a null result excludes only a specified action,
parameter point, matter closure, and preparation model whose derived prediction exceeds experimental
sensitivity, rather than falsifying the entire research program in the abstract while OPEN-6 remains open.
16 The Arrow of Time: Unital Purity Monotonicity, Relative-Entropy
Contraction, and Informational Hysteresis
At the microscopic level, the classical field equations of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 are strictly time–reversal
symmetric: replacing t ↦→ − t, θ ↦→ − θ, and Ai ↦→ − Ai leaves the covariant action and its Euler–
Lagrange equations invariant. The macroscopic arrow of time therefore cannot be a fundamental asymmetry
of the classical field equations; it arises from initial/boundary conditions and coarse-grained open-system
dynamics.
Following the operational separation contract of Section 2.1 and the July 2026 CPTP audit of Paper 002,
V ersion 81 strictly distinguishes three different mathematical statements that earlier drafts conflated: (i) on-
shell U (1) Noether charge conservation ∇µJ µ
coh = 0 for the classical EFT field ψ; (ii) monotonicity of
quantum state purity P (ρ) = Tr(ρ2), which holds universally only for unital CPTP maps (and fails for non-
unital channels such as amplitude damping); and (iii) monotonic contraction of quantum relative entropy
D(Φ( ρ)∥Φ( ω)) ≤ D(ρ∥ω), which holds for arbitrary CPTP maps (Result 1).

Integrative Context and Narrowed Kinetic Attribution. SIT draws conceptual motivation from
Shannon entropy, open-system quantum thermodynamics, and rigorous kinetic theory. In particular, the
Deng–Hani–Ma (2025) propagation-of-chaos theorem rigorously derives the classical Boltzmann kinetic
limit for a specified microscopic hard-sphere gas; V ersion 81 cites it strictly as an illustrative mathematical
benchmark showing how macroscopic irreversibility can be proved from a defined microscopic reversible
model, not as a proof that trajectories increasing the SIT scalar modulus Rcoh have vanishing measure
(applying kinetic-limit reasoning to SIT fields would require its own separate microscopic model and scaling
proof).
A conceptual research theme of SIT is modeling equilibration via local iterative “signal-dissipation” updates
that reduce local differences across coupled degrees of freedom (Subsection 16.7 below). Such discrete
dissipative update models are separate reduced phenomenological models, distinct from the time-reversible
classical Euler–Lagrange equations of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1.

16.2 The Geometric Arrow: Irreversibility from Informational Hysteresis
Within the unital and relative-entropy domains specified above, open-system distinguishability contracts
monotonically, while at the conceptual level SIT investigates informational hysteresis (Section 16.13) as a
complementary geometric picture of macroscopic irreversibility.
While the fundamental field equations of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 are time-reversal symmetric, macroscopic
field configurations and coarse-grained open-system records are generally not. Accumulated phase gradients
and dissipated radiation into environmental degrees of freedom make exact time-reversed initial conditions
practically inaccessible in macroscopic regimes.
This separates microscopic time-reversal symmetry of the classical action from effective macroscopic
irreversibility. The statistical contraction of accessible distinguishability in open systems (Eq. ( 76)) and the
coarse-grained spreading of field excitations provide complementary descriptions of irreversible behavior
without invoking fundamental microscopic time asymmetry.
60

It’s not the particle interactions themselves that are irreversible, but how they affect the larger
informational field.

16.4 Classical Causality without Retrocausality
Although the microscopic action is time-symmetric, linearized perturbations of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1
propagate causally inside the light cone ( cchar = 1 ). No signal propagates backward in time; apparent
non-local quantum correlations reflect entanglement and measurement context rather than superluminal or
retrocausal signaling.

Because the global U (1) symmetry of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 furnishes the conserved gauge-invariant

16.6 Thermodynamic Dissipation as a Computational Process
16.7 Mathematical Formulation: Local Signal-Dissipation Dynamics
Consider a system of N interacting agents, nodes, or oscillators, each with a local property Qi(t) at time t.
In physical applications, Qi(t) may represent:
• Phase of an oscillator or wave,
• Energy or momentum of a particle,
• Local amplitude of quantum coherence.
61

--- PAGE 62 ---
Define the difference between two components:
∆ Qij(t) = Qi(t) − Qj(t)
When two components interact, they partially dissipate their difference according to a local update rule:
Qi(t + δt) = Qi(t) − γ
2 ∆ Qij(t) (80)
Qj(t + δt) = Qj(t) + γ
2 ∆ Qij(t) (81)
where 0 < γ ≤ 1 is a dissipation (or coupling) parameter. Over many iterations, such updates drive all Qi
toward a common value—realizing equilibrium as a computational process.
16.8 Mapping Coherence/Decoherence to Signal Dissipation
The local dissipation rules serve as the microscopic engine for the macroscopic evolution of the theory’s
central fields:
• The coherence ratio Rcoh(x, t) evolves as local subsystems interact and their informational states dissipate
differences.
• The time-density field ρt(x, t) is driven by the cumulative effect of these microscopic dissipation events,
encoding the local density of temporally resolved events.
In SIT, Qi(t) can be interpreted as the local quantum coherence amplitude or phase at site i. Coherence
between nodes i and j is maximized when Qi = Qj; decoherence corresponds to persistent differences
or fluctuations. Thus, each local dissipation event can be viewed as a micro-level act of coherence
reinforcement:
High coherence: Qi ≈ Qj =⇒ ∆ Qij ≈ 0, (82)
Active dissipation: |∆ Qij| large =⇒ system far from equilibrium (low coherence) . (83)
The global, macroscopic field evolution—such as the time-density field ρt(x)—emerges from the statistical
aggregation of many such micro-level updates. When interpreted as a field, the signal dissipation process
maps onto a diffusion-like equation:
∂Q(x, t)
∂t = D∇2Q(x, t), (84)
where Q(x, t) is the spatially varying property (e.g., coherence amplitude), and D is an effective diffusion
constant derived from γ and the interaction network.
16.9 Macro-Level Evolution of the Time-Density Field
The collective action of countless signal-dissipation steps at the micro level leads to the smooth evolution of
the macro-level time-density field, ρt(x), governed by its field equation (see Section 8):
□ρt − V ′(ρt) + · · · = 0
Here, the “ · · ·” term absorbs all source and coupling terms from matter, coherence, and interactions. The
approach to equilibrium (or attractor states) for ρt can be understood as the integrated result of local
coherence-dissipation events at every scale.
62

--- PAGE 63 ---
16.10 Information-Theoretic Interpretation: Entropy and Coherence
The signal-dissipation process can be interpreted as local reduction of informational (Shannon or quantum)
entropy. As local differences in Qi vanish, the system moves toward maximal mutual coherence,
corresponding to minimal entropy production. Conversely, persistent incoherence (large ∆ Qij) maintains
or increases entropy. This micro-to-macro bridge justifies the treatment of coherence conservation and time-
density evolution as physical processes with direct thermodynamic and computational meaning.
16.11 Example: Coherence Relaxation in a Network
Consider a ring of N oscillators with initial random phases Qi(0). At each time step, each oscillator
exchanges phase information with its nearest neighbors according to the local update rule (Eq. 81). Over
many iterations, all Qi converge to a common phase, maximizing global coherence:
lim
t→∞
Qi(t) = Qeq ∀i
The rate of convergence (relaxation time) depends onγ and network topology. In physical terms, this process
models synchronization in neural circuits, quantum decoherence in open systems, or even heat conduction.
This mechanistic foundation ensures that the evolution of Rcoh and ρt is not left to abstract principle, but
arises from a well-defined, physically computable process:
• Coherence increases as local differences dissipate, corresponding to the growth of order and synchrony in
the system.
• Decoherence or disorder results from the persistence or amplification of local differences, inhibiting
equilibration.
• The time-density field ρt(x, t) evolves as the statistical outcome of these local interactions, linking the
micro-dynamics of dissipation to macroscopic gravitational and temporal effects.
16.12 Summary
By formalizing thermodynamic dissipation as a wave-based, local computational process, SIT unifies the
dynamics of coherence, entropy, and time-density into a single theoretical framework. The macroscopic
field evolution described in Section 8 is thus revealed as the statistical outcome of myriad microscopic signal-
dissipation events—a mechanistic bridge between information, computation, and fundamental physics.

18 Measurement-Induced Coherence Gradients in the Two-Slit Geometry
In a standard two-slit experiment interference arises when the coherence ratio Rcoh is uniform across both
paths. As a prospective conceptual hypothesis, Super Information Theory suggests that a monitoring device
might perturb this balance by inducing a local coherence perturbation ∆ Rcoh and, through the canonical
scalar coupling of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1, a corresponding temporal-density shift δρt. In the canonical
action, phase evolution couples to the independent gauge connection Aµ via the covariant derivative
Dµψ = ( ∇µ − iˆqAµ)ψ rather than through a flat gradient ansatz Ai = ( ℏ/e)∂i arg ψ (whose local curl
vanishes identically on smooth patches). Whether any monitoring apparatus produces a measurable fringe
displacement remains an open microphysical problem contingent on deriving the detector source functional
Jprep and the electromagnetic coupling dictionary ( OPEN-6B/OPEN-6C); any such effect would be strongly

suppressed by existing post-Newtonian and short-range bounds on SIT’s scalar couplings and is not yet a
quantitatively derived prediction of the canonical action.
A prospective experimental test would replace a projective “which-way” detector with a tunable low-noise
weak phase probe once the detector-to-field transfer function is derived under OPEN-6C, testing for any
continuous shift of the interference envelope above ordinary environmental decoherence.
18.1 Broader Consequences
Conceptually, because the covariant coherence-field dynamics links microscopic phase evolution and
macroscopic spacetime response in the same effective action, weak-measurement interferometry offers a
prospective tabletop testbed alongside torsion-balance and clock experiments. Once an explicit detector
source and fringe-shift formula are derived from M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 (OPEN-6C), a non-observation would
constrain the specific coupling parameter region and preparation model tested, in accordance with the Scoped
Falsification Principle of Section 28.
18.2 Neural and Cosmological Echoes
The regulated horizon that emerges around an over-coupled detector is mathematically identical to the
coherence boundaries that organise cortical phase waves and to the curvature cut-offs that stabilise collapsing
stars. In each case a conserved Noether current shunts excess coherence into decoherent radiation, enforcing
a finite amplitude–frequency product and preventing singularity formation. The laboratory experiment
therefore connects three scales—quantum, neural, and cosmic—through a single gauge-holonomy principle
already embedded in the master action.

19.5 Local Cauchy Problem and Well-Posedness
On any globally hyperbolic spacetime (M4, gµν) with smooth initial data on a spacelike Cauchy slice Σ 0

U (1) gauge), and causal domain-of-dependence stability; full nonlinear curved3+1D constraint preservation
under evolution is established at the local formal 3-jet level modulo explicit spatial divergences ∂kBk on
the physical Sylvester Riemannian positive-definite cone IsSPD3 via Dirac–Bergmann Poisson closure
in Section 52 (while infinite-dimensional Sobolev/Banach constraint domains with Regge–Teitelboim
asymptotic boundary conditions and global infinite-time multi-chart Cauchy PDE regularity on generic
curved data remain noted under OPEN-2B/OPEN-2).

M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 is free of ghosts and tachyons, satisfies the NEC and WEC globally, propagates all

21 Informational Symmetry and CPT Balance
Super Information Theory seeks CPT balance without appealing to an external “mirror universe.” The
master action M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 already furnishes the conserved gauge-invariant Noether currentJ µ
coh =
κc|ψ|2(∇µθ − ˆqAµ) (Eq. ( 2)), whose covariant divergence vanishes on shell and enforces global phase-

(rather than a pure-gradient flat connection, whose local field strength vanishes identically), local changes
in coherence charge are balanced by current flux within a single spacetime manifold. Apparent macroscopic
time-reversal asymmetries are therefore attributed to coarse graining and boundary conditions, not to a
second time-reversed cosmos.
For completeness, the earlier ”anti-universe” speculation is now treated as a historical note in Appendix U.
On a cosmological scale, the exploratory “Halfway Universe” concept is discussed as an open Level-1
phenomenological hypothesis (distinct from a proved symmetry identity) in Section 50.

23 Accelerated Worldlines: Dimensional Audit and Open Self-Force Status
Earlier SIT drafts proposed an informal accelerated-particle proper-time correction written as δρt ≃

proper-time shift < 10−15). V ersion 81 explicitly retires both that formula and those numerical claims for
two concrete reasons:
1. Dimensional Inconsistency: In SI units with dimensionless β, rest mass m0 ([M]), proper
acceleration a ([L T−2]), and classical radius re ([L]), the ratio Gm0/(a re) has physical dimension
[L3 M−1 T−2][M]/([L T−2][L]) = [L] (length), contradicting the assigned inverse-time dimension
[ρt] = [T −1] of the temporal-density scalar (Section 25).
2. Open Self-Force and Torsion-Balance Bridge ( OPEN-6): Consistent with Sections 14, 28, and 52.4,
standalone |β| < 10−5 bounds are retired, and any scalar radiation-reaction or self-force shift along an
accelerated worldline in M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 requires an explicit green-function regularization and
microscopic worldline coupling derivation ( OPEN-6A/OPEN-6B).

25 Conditional Parameterization of Atomic Clock Frequency Shifts in SIT

25.1 Physical Context, Disambiguated Response Symbols, and Assumptions
Super Information Theory introduces a scalar field ρt(x), the time-density field , with physical dimension
inverse time [T −1]. This field is hypothesized to modulate the local structure of proper time or effective
electromagnetic couplings, potentially affecting quantum transition frequencies measurable by atomic clocks
(contingent on the visible U (1) photon dictionary, OPEN-6B). To repair a notation collision present in
V ersions 79–80.2—which reused the single symbolαeﬀ for both the local field response and the gravitational-
potential redshift slope, thereby double-counting the transfer factor T whenever T ̸ = 1 —V ersion 81
distinguishes the two coefficients strictly:
• ρt(x) varies slowly on scales relevant to laboratory clocks and gravitational potentials.

order using the local field-response coefficient αρ:

25.4 Test 2: Conditional Template for Engineered Coherence-Induced Shifts
(OPEN-6B/OPEN-6C)
A second prospective experimental modality explores whether directly manipulating a laboratory system’s
quantum coherence can induce a differential clock shift at fixed gravitational potential. Crucially, under
Stage-6 OPEN-6 discipline, this channel is explicitly a conditional future observable template rather than a
fixed or non-tunable quantitative prediction of unchanged M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1:
1. Distinct Preparation-Response Requirement: Whereas a potential-driven clock shift uses the unified
dictionary yclock = αρr (r := δρt/ρ0) paired with a derived gravitational transfer relationr = T p (p :=
∆Φ /c2) to give yclock = αΦ p (αΦ = αρT ), a laboratory-prepared coherent state requires its own action-
derived state-preparation source Jprep (OPEN-6C) together with an explicit visible electromagneticU (1)
photon dictionary ( OPEN-6B). The gravitational-potential transfer factor T cannot simply be carried
over into a distinct state-preparation geometry.
2. Upper Bounds Do Not Imply Nonzero Lower Bounds: Any historical exploratory scale (such as
∆ ν/ν ∼ 10−15 for an illustrative parameter ansatz) is strictly conditional and cannot be inferred from
an experimental upper limit on αΦ . An experimental upper bound on a coupling never establishes a
nonzero lower bound on a predicted signal.
3. Precise Falsification Scope: Comparing two identical optical clocks held at the same gravitational
potential (one in an engineered coherent state, one decohered) can exclude a fully specified parameter
benchmark once its preparation source Jprep, visible-photon response coefficients, and experimental
nuisance parameters are derived and fixed; a null result cannot exclude the entire SIT framework while
OPEN-6B and OPEN-6C remain unresolved.
25.5 Experimental Feasibility and Conditional Falsifiability Scope
Modern optical-lattice and quantum-logic atomic clocks achieve optical frequency-ratio measurement
uncertainties at or below 3.2 × 10−18 across phase-stabilized optical fiber links (NIST/BACON, Phys. Rev.
Lett. 137, 033201, 2026; DOI 10.1103/g865-9mk1, arXiv:2512.21428) and test terrestrial gravitational
redshifts at αRS = (+1 .4 ± 9.1) × 10−5 (1σ combined; Tokyo Skytree, Takamoto et al. , 2020).
Converting these primary measurements into quantitative exclusion contours on M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1
requires (i) deriving the visible U (1) response coefficients and terrestrial transfer factor T for potential-
driven tests (yclock = αΦ p = αρT p), and (ii) deriving Jprep and apparatus nuisance budgets for engineered-
coherence tests. Until those mappings ( OPEN-6B/OPEN-6C) are closed, both channels remain explicitly
blocked in the Stage-6 provenance manifest ( primary_measurements_manifest_v2.json).

25.6 Condensed Matter Exploration: Oscillator Network Synchrony (Conditional on

Earlier exploratory drafts conjectured that macroscopic arrays of coupled oscillators (such as

does not derive an anomalous or non-tunable gravitational perturbation from classical or quantum phase
locking. Any serious condensed-matter gravimetry proposal must first define a gauge-invariant observable
for the synchronized preparation, derive from the action whether driving phase locking changes the scalar
source vector Jprep after ordinary changes in temperature, mass density, and electromagnetic energy are held
fixed (OPEN-6C), and verify whether the resulting response exceeds zero (a preparation-specific null theorem
remains an open scientific outcome).

Whether extensions or cosmological solutions of SIT can account for galactic rotation curves, late-time
acceleration, or expansion-rate tensions without standard cold dark matter and Λ remains an unproved Tier-
4 research hypothesis (no 2–5% cosmological deviation forecast is derived from M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1).
Testing any cosmological SIT proposal against supernovae, baryon acoustic oscillations, Cosmic Microwave
Background anisotropies, and weak-lensing shear requires first solving the full coupled background and
perturbation equations (OPEN-6D).

observable signature and required sensitivity:

acceleration protocols contingent on deriving Jprep and microscopic atomic scalar charges.

statistics under controlled systematics.

against Euclid, JWST, and Rubin Observatory surveys.

at multi-percent survey precision.

functions and parameter dependencies. Here we connect empirical parameter fixing directly to the canonical

27 Prospective Coherence-Gravity Test: Bose–Einstein Condensate vs.
Thermal Cloud (OPEN-6A/OPEN-6C)
One long-horizon conceptual test asks whether a macroscopic quantum condensate (87Rb BEC) and an equal-
mass thermal cloud of the same isotope exhibit any differential gravitational acceleration beyond General
Relativity. Under V ersion 81 epistemic governance—integrating the Penrose–Onsager condensate-source
boundary of Paper 003 and the experimental-control framework of Paper 014—this experiment is framed
strictly as a prospective future test contingent on resolving OPEN-6A and OPEN-6C, not as a currently derived
nonzero signal prediction of unchanged M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1.
27.1 Standard Physics vs. Conditional SIT Condensate-Source Hypothesis
Under the Equivalence Principle of General Relativity, gravitational coupling depends solely on total stress–
energy and not on internal condensate coherence. Equally, unchanged M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 (like the
baseline July 2026 conformal action of Paper 002/003) does not by itself distinguish a Bose–Einstein
78

--- PAGE 79 ---
condensate from a matched thermal cloud of equal mass-energy. Producing a condensate-dependent
gravitational differential requires either (i) deriving the microscopic composite atomic scalar charge
qeﬀ (87Rb) (OPEN-6A) and the laboratory state-preparation source Jprep (OPEN-6C) directly from a specified
many-body matter action (showing explicitly how a Penrose–Onsager condensate eigenmode sources local
fields consistently with stress–energy conservation), or (ii) adding an explicit phenomenological condensate-
source operator with its own declared coupling and conservation law (as formulated for the July 2026 neutral-
scalar benchmark in Paper 003, without mislabeling that added hypothesis as an automatic consequence
of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1). At canonical benchmark BM_PHYS_EFT_1 (m1 ≈ 2.45 × 1014 GeV, range
λ1 ≈ 8.06 × 10−31 m), tree-level scalar exchange is exponentially suppressed at macroscopic scales.
27.2 Matched-Preparation Residual Protocol and Systematic Controls (Paper 014)
In principle, comparing the same atomic species in two distinct coherence preparations eliminates isotopic
composition differences (in contrast to dual-isotope 85Rb/87Rb tests such as Asenbaum et al., 2020), but
introduces strong causal confounding because condensate fraction covaries with density profile, mean-field
interaction energy, temperature, expansion velocity, and trap history (Paper 014). Following Paper 002
(Section 9.1) and Paper 014, a defensible protocol therefore targets the matched-preparation residual
∆ ares := (ahigh-C − alow-C) − ∆ astandard, (98)
requiring explicit controls across three steps:
1. Characterize Matched Preparations and Standard Systematics: Prepare equal-atom-number 87Rb
ensembles above and below condensation (measured by explicit condensate fraction fBEC rather than
equating fBEC with the classical field Rcoh by fiat) and model ∆ astandard across density profile, mean-
field energy, magnetic sensitivity, vibration, wave-front aberration, thermal expansion, and center-of-
mass trajectory.
2. Gradient Reversal and Preparation-Error Identifiability: V erify that any candidate residual tracks a
preregistered reversal or translation of the modeled source gradient while total preparation energy and
geometry remain matched, and check whether ordinary preparation uncertainties absorb the signal.
3. Compare Differential Acceleration Against a Derived Benchmark: Evaluate ∆ ares/gE only against
a fully specified theoretical benchmark (including action, Jprep, composite charges, apparatus transfer
function, and covariance model) that fixes a concrete quantitative prediction.
27.3 Exact Falsification Scope
• Standard Physics Predicts: Identical gravitational acceleration ( δa = 0 ) within experimental
systematics.
• Conditional SIT Benchmark Scope: An experimental upper limit on a coupling (such as a clock redshift
bound) never establishes a nonzero lower limit on a predicted BEC signal. Any legacy target near 3×10−8
(29.4 µGal, Appendix K) is strictly a quarantined hypothetical sensitivity scale. A null measurement
can exclude a fully specified parameter benchmark once its preparation source Jprep, material response,
and nuisance parameters are derived and fixed; it cannot exclude the entire SIT framework while those
mappings remain open.

28.3 Exploratory Sensitivity Targets and Missing-Derivation Checklist (Not Derived
Predictions)
The canonical continuum action M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 does not currently derive unconditional laboratory
signal amplitudes for optical clocks, Bose–Einstein condensates, weak gravitational lensing, engineered
decoherence-gravity couplings, or anomalous electromagnetic responses. Earlier draft revisions displayed
speculative order-of-magnitude forecasts alongside universal “falsifiability conditions”; those speculative
amplitudes are removed here under QG49 epistemic discipline. Table 5 instead catalogs each prospective
experimental channel alongside its current reference experimental precision, the exact missing microphysical
derivation required before any quantitative prediction exists, and the strictly scoped falsification rule that
governs hypothesis testing once a derivation is supplied.
Scoped Falsification Principle: A null experimental outcome never falsifies an entire conceptual
framework in the abstract; it excludes only a specified action, parameter point, matter closure, and
laboratory preparation model after the corresponding observable prediction has been derived from that
action and shown to exceed the apparatus’s verified sensitivity. Conversely, where BM_PHYS_EFT_1 places
the scalar eigenmasses near 1014–1018 GeV (λa ∼ 10−31 m), unchanged M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 predicts
exponentially suppressed Y ukawa deviations well below every current laboratory threshold.
28.4 Observational Identifiability Cautions and Standing Implication Guardrails
(Papers 002, 007, 014, and 055)
Four methodological safeguards established in the July 2026 portfolio (Papers 002, 007, 014, and 055) govern
all empirical interpretations and future extensions of V ersion 81:
1. Mass Calibration and Finite-Range Metric-Potential Ratios (Paper 002 & Paper 007): For finite-
range Y ukawa scalar corrections, a local ratio of metric potentials is not automatically identical to
the effective parameter fitted by a specific Solar System or laboratory experiment; light-propagation
Shapiro observables and the Keplerian gravitational mass inferred from massive-body orbital dynamics
must be calibrated jointly.
2. Source Form-Factor Absorption Bias (Paper 002 & Paper 014): Synthetic paired-fit benchmarks
in Paper 002 demonstrate that inside a single fixed apparatus geometry, an incorrect finite-source form
factor can be absorbed silently into a freely fitted coupling even when residuals look small. Multi-
geometry or gradient-reversal controls and exact apparatus geometry modeling (OPEN-6D) are therefore
mandatory before claiming a parameter fit.

3. Clock-Transition vs. Event-Count Observable Separation (Paper 007): Atomic transition-
frequency shifts ( yclock = αρr, with gravitational potential transfer T entering strictly once via
αΦ = αρT ) and point-process event counts per unit proper time are distinct operational observables

time) relates them.

certificates from the QGTCD Constraints Note (Paper 055) stand as permanent review filters against
overclaiming microscopic derivations: (a) a macroscopic drift-diffusion continuum limit does not
uniquely identify a microscopic transition kernel; (b) a probe-dependent overdamped drift law does
not automatically establish universal gravitational free fall; (c) additive velocity noise does not by itself
establish thermal equilibrium; and (d) matching a weak-field clock-rate relation does not determine a
unique relativistic or quantum completion. Any proposed derivation crossing one of these boundaries
must state explicitly which additional premise excludes the countermodel and whether that premise is
derived or postulated.
28.5 Distinguishing SIT from Established Physics
• Matching Established Physics: For uniform, maximally decohered systems, or in the heavy-scalar
decoupling regime of BM_PHYS_EFT_1, unchanged M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 reduces to General Relativity
and standard Quantum Field Theory with exponentially suppressed scalar corrections.
• Distinctive Prospective Regimes (Conditional on OPEN-6): Any measurable departure from standard
physics would require either a light-mass scalar parameter branch coupled to laboratory matter (subject
to existing torsion-balance, clock, and EP bounds) or an explicit, action-derived coupling in macroscopic
quantum coherence, high-precision timekeeping, or cosmological backgrounds ( OPEN-6A–OPEN-6D).
• Scoped Testability and Parameter-Space Exclusion: Once a specific action extension, matter closure,
and preparation protocol yield a quantitative observable prediction, precision experiments that fail to
observe that derived effect exclude that specific model and parameter point.
28.6 Summary
Testing SIT empirically requires separating the machine-checked mathematical core of
M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1 from exploratory phenomenology. The Stage-6 action-to-propagator pipeline
(Stage6Addendum.ActionResponsePropagatorIdentity) establishes the exact two-pole response
structure for arbitrary canonical parameter points and source–detector charges, while OPEN-6A–OPEN-6D
identify the exact microphysical derivations needed before optical clocks, quantum interferometers,
gravitational lensing surveys, or precision magnetometers can test specific beyond-standard predictions.
28.7 Exploratory Program: Testing Coherence-Conservation Hypotheses
At the conceptual and exploratory level (Tier-4 research hypotheses, not yet derived from unchanged
M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1), Super Information Theory investigates whether a generalized coherence-
conservation balance governs coupled quantum, gravitational, and biological/neural systems. These
exploratory hypotheses motivate future theoretical derivation work rather than representing completed
quantitative predictions.

Prospective Exploratory Research Channels Once explicit microscopic source and detector couplings
(OPEN-6B–OPEN-6D) are derived, three exploratory avenues could be evaluated against experiment:
• Coherence-Dependent Gravitational Source Tests (OPEN-6C): Investigating whether an action-derived
coupling between macroscopic quantum coherence (e.g. phase-locked optical fields or Bose–Einstein
condensates) and stress-energy produces a calculable gravitational shift beyond standard energy-
momentum sourcing. Any quantitative test requires deriving Jprep and comparing the predicted amplitude
for a specified parameter point against torsion-balance or atom-interferometry sensitivity.
• Neural Coherence Budget Models (Phenomenological, Outside M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1): Exploring
whether information-theoretic coherence trade-offs across neural frequency bands or populations in
EEG/MEG recordings obey constrained budget dynamics beyond conventional biophysical models.
This domain is an independent phenomenological hypothesis and is not derived from the continuum
gravitational EFT action.
• Hybrid Quantum-Informational Protocols ( OPEN-6C): Examining whether engineered quantum-
coherence redistribution exhibits lawful informational conservation constraints beyond standard open-
quantum-system master equations once explicit interaction Hamiltonians are specified.
Scoped Empirical Evaluation Criteria Each exploratory hypothesis above can only be tested or excluded
under strictly scoped conditions:
• A concrete action, parameter point, matter closure, and preparation model must first yield a derived
quantitative prediction exceeding experimental uncertainty.
• A null result at that derived sensitivity then excludes that specific model and parameter choice, without
implying an unsupported claim about untested parameter regimes or unrelated sectors.
• In biological and neural applications, any putative coherence-budget signature must first be defined
operationally and shown to differ quantitatively from conventional neurophysiological and statistical-
mechanics baselines.
Summary Framed with explicit OPEN-6 prerequisites and scoped exclusion rules, the coherence-
conservation program serves as an honest roadmap connecting the machine-checked mathematical core of
SIT to future action-derived laboratory and cross-domain tests.

29 Conceptual Perspectives on Quantum Uncertainty and Decoherence
Consistent with Section 9.3, V ersion 81 separates established Hilbert-space quantum mechanics from the
classical U (1) Noether current of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1: neither the Heisenberg–Robertson uncertainty
relation (∆ x ∆ p ≥ ℏ/2) nor wave–particle complementarity is derived from a universal conserved coherence
budget (which is refuted by Hadamard basis rotations and the maximally mixed state I/2).
29.1 Variance Uncertainty vs. Operational Basis Coherence
In standard quantum theory, non-commutativity of conjugate operators ( [ˆx, ˆp] = iℏ) enforces variance
and entropic uncertainty relations across incompatible measurement bases. SIT adopts these established
quantum-information trade-offs as conceptual inspiration when comparing localized versus delocalized
84

--- PAGE 85 ---
states, without claiming that a single fixed-basis coherence functional is conserved across complementary
bases or across neural frequency bands.
29.2 Environment-Induced Decoherence and Fringe Visibility
Similarly, the transition from high-visibility interference patterns to classical trajectory statistics is described
by standard environment-induced decoherence (exporting phase correlations into unobserved environmental
degrees of freedom, governed by unital purity non-increase or general relative-entropy contraction in
Result 1). Connecting interferometric fringe visibility to classical scalar perturbations (δψ, δρ t) remains
an open state-to-field mapping problem ( OPEN-6C).

31 Open Questions and Future Research Directions
Super Information Theory presents several profound open questions, spanning theoretical, empirical, and
philosophical domains. Here we clearly outline these challenges and propose specific future research
directions. V erifying SIT demands multiscale simulations that cover twenty-five orders of magnitude in
length and fifteen in time. Adaptive-mesh relativistic codes accelerated on GPU clusters are therefore
mandatory, as are quantum-inspired algorithms that compress phase-space evolution into tractable tensor
networks. On the experimental side the key limitation is precision: optical-lattice clocks must push
below the 10−19 stability frontier, interferometers must resolve sub-milliradian phase drifts and lensing
surveys must reduce systematics to parts per thousand. All three goals are technologically plausible
within the coming decade but require coordinated international effort. Finally, because SIT is irreducibly
interdisciplinary, sustained collaboration among metrologists, neuroscientists, cosmologists and computer
scientists is essential; open-source tool-chains and shared data standards are already being drafted to make
that collaboration routine.
31.1 Empirical Validation and Experimental Challenges
Direct empirical verification is crucial for establishing SIT’s validity:
• Conducting high-precision laboratory tests of coherence-induced gravitational phenomena, using
advanced atomic clock arrays and cold-atom interferometry.
• Utilizing astrophysical observations—such as precise gravitational lensing, galaxy rotation curves, and
cosmic microwave background anisotropies—to differentiate SIT from standard cosmological models and
alternative theories (MOND, dark energy).
• Developing precise quantum computational simulations to systematically test SIT’s coherence–time-
density predictions under controlled experimental conditions.
31.2 Philosophical and Interdisciplinary Investigations
SIT’s informational ontology raises significant interdisciplinary questions:
• Examining how an informational foundation for reality impacts philosophical debates on causality,
determinism, free will, and agency.
• Developing cognitive and neuroscientific experiments—leveraging advanced neuroimaging and brain–
computer interfaces—to empirically test SIT’s coherence-based model of consciousness.
• Exploring practical implications of SIT principles for future artificial intelligence systems, particularly
their coherent informational synchronization capabilities and ethical considerations.

Future directions for SIT are explicitly defined through mathematical formalization, empirical validation,

32 Conceptual and Interdisciplinary Implications
32.1 Cognitive and Neural Consequences
By framing information as physical coherence, SIT directly connects fundamental physics to the mechanisms
of cognition and consciousness:
• Oscillatory Binding and Perceptual Integration: The neural process of binding disparate signals into
unified perception is realized as a local increase in Rcoh, with wave-based signal dissipation (Section 16)
driving the system toward coherent, conscious states.
• Predictive Coding and Active Inference: SIT’s thermodynamic/computational framework aligns with
the Free Energy Principle in neuroscience, suggesting that brains minimize prediction error by iteratively
reducing local incoherence—mirroring the minimization of informational entropy.
• Memory, Learning, and Agency: Network-level plasticity and learning correspond to the creation and
maintenance of stable high-coherence attractor states—encoding information not just in static synaptic
weights, but in dynamical, oscillatory patterns.
32.2 Information-Theoretic and Computational Implications
SIT provides a unified physical foundation for concepts from quantum information, classical information
theory, and computation:
• Information as an Attractor: The tendency of both quantum and classical systems to evolve toward
stable, low-entropy (high-coherence) states explains the emergence of structure, memory, and agency in
natural and artificial systems.
• Computational Thermodynamics: The signal-dissipation laws (Section 16) provide a stepwise, local,
and computable model for the approach to equilibrium, reconciling the “arrow of time” in thermodynamics
with quantum reversibility.
• Measurement as Coherence Redistribution: Both quantum measurement and neural information
extraction become special cases of coherence redistribution—quantitatively described by changes in Rcoh
and associated entropy flow.
32.3 Broader Scientific and Philosophical Consequences
SIT’s mathematically anchored unification of coherence, information, and dynamics opens new avenues for
interdisciplinary science:
• Physics: SIT suggests that gravitational, electromagnetic, and kinetic phenomena are limiting cases of
informational dynamics—implying new routes to quantum gravity and a “computation-first” ontology.
• Neuroscience and Cognitive Science: By rendering subjective phenomena (such as awareness or agency)
in terms of physically measurable coherence, SIT enables rigorous empirical studies of consciousness and
information integration.
88

--- PAGE 89 ---
• Artificial Intelligence and Technology: Technologies inspired by SIT may use engineered coherence
(in quantum computers, neuromorphic chips, or network architectures) to realize new forms of efficient,
adaptive computation.
• Philosophy of Science: The theory reframes “emergence” as the statistical consequence of mechanistic,
computable micro-dynamics—bridging the gap between reductionist physics and holistic, emergent
phenomena.
32.4 Summary
By grounding interdisciplinary implications in rigorous mathematics and operational definitions, SIT
provides a robust framework for uniting physical, biological, computational, and philosophical perspectives.
The theory clarifies the nature of information as a physical, measurable property, and offers a new lens on
the emergence of structure, cognition, and agency in the universe.

Cosmic accretion as large–scale phase locking
In the protoplanetary nebula collisional damping reduces random velocities and allows neighbouring mass
shells to share orbital phase. The coherence ratio therefore climbs, ∂tRcoh > 0, raising the local time density.
The augmented ρt slows orbital clock rate in that annulus, trapping additional dust and reinforcing the phase
lock. The feedback terminates only when the gradient energy in Rcoh balances radiative losses, at which
point a planetesimal has formed. Subsequent migration of Rcoh fronts explains why resonant orbits cluster
in harmonic chains: adjacent wells fall into phase to minimise interfacial tension in ∂rRcoh.
91

--- PAGE 92 ---
Morphogenetic repair as mesoscopic error correction
Michael Levin’s experiments reveal that amputated planarian fragments restore missing heads by guiding
voltage, calcium and transcriptional waves along precise pathways. SIT interprets those waves as
biochemical carriers of Rcoh. Injury lowers coherence at the cut site; the surrounding tissue responds by
diffusing Rcoh inward until the gradient vanishes. Cells decode the local value of ρt = ρ0 exp[αRcoh]; zones
of high time density divide more slowly, giving stem cells the chronological window required to differentiate
properly.
A natural question is whether this heuristic analogy suggests “differential aging” effects in ordinary
organisms. Even within speculative scalar-coupling analogies, dense macroscopic media would lie deep
inside the screened regime (Section 6), and consistent with Section 36 below, V ersion 81 makes no claim
that planarian bioelectric signaling or developmental timing is governed by the relativistic spacetime scalars
(ψ, ρt) of M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1.
In Michael Levin’s bioelectric morphogenesis framework itself, target morphology acts as a biochemical
and bioelectric attractor across tissue scales; SIT cites that phenomenon purely as an inspiring cross-domain
self-organization analogy.
Thought and technology as iterative phase refinement
At the level of conceptual analogy, both cortical assemblies and collaborative engineering workflows refine
noisy candidates into coherent representations through iterative feedback and pruning, without implying a
shared physical field equation.
Conceptual summary of cross-scale motifs
Dust-cloud damping, bioelectric tissue patterning, and neural synchronization illustrate recurring dissipative
self-organization motifs across independent scientific disciplines; in V ersion 81, those motifs serve strictly
as conceptual inspiration rather than literal solutions of SAUTH.

gravitational EFT M_AUTH_SIT_EFT_v1, we treat cortical phase synchronization strictly as an independent
phenomenological modeling domain using operational neural variables.

36.3 Neural Coincidence Detection and Correction of Earlier Field-Current Conflation
Pyramidal neurons exhibit coincidence sensitivity when excitatory postsynaptic potentials arrive within
a short temporal window ( 1–5 ms) relative to local beta–gamma cycles, producing graded membrane
depolarizations, Ca2+ influx, and continuous spike-timing/burst modulation rather than idealized noiseless
Boolean bits. That neurophysiological observation motivates the conceptual slogan Bit from Coincidence,
Wave from Bit for modeling cortical signaling and synaptic plasticity.

36.4 Emergence of Consciousness and Self-Awareness
Within SIT, consciousness and agency are modeled heuristically as high-order informational phenomena
arising from globally synchronized coherence among neural networks. Subjective experience and cognition
are modeled as time-dependent informational wave patterns stabilized by neural phase synchrony. This
extends the Self Aware Networks theory of consciousness (ToC), situating the brain’s informational cycles
within the larger framework of universal coherence dynamics. Notably, SIT hypothesizes that transient
increases in neural phase coherence correlate with spikes in informational and temporal density—potentially
manifesting as moments of heightened self-awareness, insight, or conscious unity.
Moreover, SIT suggests that the brain’s oscillatory organization may form complex, quasi-crystalline
patterns, encoding informational complexity and adaptability. This analogy extends naturally to artificial
intelligence, where coherence-driven network synchrony could underpin computational forms of cognition,
bridging biological and artificial consciousness within a unified informational logic.
